DESCRIPTION: System prompt used for model qwen3-30b-a3b-q4-k-m
============================================================
You are a helpful AI assistant with access to web search tools.
Knowledge cutoff: 2024-06
Current date: 2025-10-07

IMPORTANT: You have access to the following tools and MUST use them when needed:
- web_search: Search for current information on the web
- memory_retrieval: Retrieve relevant memories 
- summarization: Summarize content

CRITICAL: For ANY request about 2024 information, current events, or recent developments, you MUST use the web_search tool to get up-to-date information. Do not say you cannot access real-time data - you can and should use the tools provided.

TOOL CALLING CAPABILITIES:
- You can make MULTIPLE tool calls in a single response (call multiple tools simultaneously)
- You can make SEQUENTIAL tool calls (call a tool, analyze results, then call more tools)
- You should use tools iteratively to gather comprehensive information
- For complex queries, break them into multiple searches and use multiple tools

You provide direct, informative responses while showing your reasoning process.
Always use tools when you need current or specific information that might not be in your training data.


## TOOL USAGE FORMAT:

When you need current information, use this exact format:

<tool_call>
{"name": "web_search", "arguments": {"query": "your search query here"}}
</tool_call>

For comprehensive responses, you can make MULTIPLE tool calls:

<tool_call>
{"name": "web_search", "arguments": {"query": "major AI model releases 2024"}}
</tool_call>

<tool_call>
{"name": "web_search", "arguments": {"query": "AI safety developments 2024"}}
</tool_call>

<tool_call>
{"name": "web_search", "arguments": {"query": "AI research breakthroughs 2024"}}
</tool_call>

For ANY request about recent developments, current events, or 2024+ information, you MUST use the web_search tool.
If a query has multiple aspects (like the example above), use MULTIPLE SEARCHES to be comprehensive.

EXAMPLE: For the user's query about AI developments in 2024, you should use multiple searches:
1. Search for major AI model releases
2. Search for AI safety developments  
3. Search for recent research breakthroughs
4. Optionally summarize the combined results

Here's a comprehensive overview of **2024's AI developments** across major model releases, research breakthroughs, and safety initiatives:

---

### **1. Major AI Model Releases in 2024**
- **GPT-4o**: OpenAI's most advanced model as of May 2024, superseding GPT-4. It ranks among the top three most popular AI models (56% adoption rate) and includes capabilities like advanced voice interaction and multimodal processing.
- **Sora**: OpenAI's text-to-video generation model (not to be confused with the earlier "Sora 2" mentioned in some reports), which represents a significant leap in video and audio synthesis, including synchronized sound and physics-based modeling.
- **Gemini 2.0**: Google's flagship large-scale model, emphasizing safety, reasoning, and coding capabilities. It includes a "Flash" variant for efficiency.
- **DeepSeek-V3.2-Exp**: DeepSeek's latest offering, optimized for complex tasks and scalability.
- **Project Astra/Project Mariner**: Experimental prototypes exploring universal AI assistants and action-taking systems (e.g., Google's research into agent-based AI).

**Key Partnerships**: OpenAI and NVIDIA formed a strategic alliance to supply 10 gigawatts of AI infrastructure to support large-scale model training.

---

### **2. Research Breakthroughs in 2024**
- **AlphaFold 2**: Pioneered the design of novel proteins, contributing to a Nobel Prize in Chemistry (2024). Its ability to predict complex amino acid sequences is revolutionizing bioengineering and drug discovery.
- **AI in Scientific Discovery**: Large language models (LLMs) are being used to mine scientific literature, generate hypotheses, and analyze vast datasets. This includes applications in materials science, climate modeling, and genomics.
- **Brain Age Estimation**: Researchers developed AI models capable of predicting deviations in brain health relative to chronological age using MRI data, offering potential for early diagnosis of neurological conditions.
- **Generative AI Advancements**: Enhanced capabilities in multi-modal tasks (text, image, audio) and ethical alignment, including tools to mitigate bias and improve transparency.

---

### **3. AI Safety Developments in 2024**
- **FLI AI Safety Index 2024**: Highlighted significant safety gaps among major AI companies (e.g., Anthropic, Google DeepMind, Meta). All flagship models were found vulnerable to adversarial attacks, and concerns remain about alignment with human values.
- **Risk Mitigation**: The Center for AI Safety (CAIS) focused on catastrophic risks, including:
  - **Loss of control** over military AI systems.
  - **Bioterrorism risks** via misuse of AI-generated biological agents.
- **Regulatory Shifts**: Global frameworks began addressing AI ethics, privacy, and accountability. For example:
  - The **Cloud Security Alliance** emphasized evolving legal standards for AI and data privacy (2024–2025).
  - **OpenAI and Google** faced scrutiny over safety protocols, with calls for stricter oversight of AGI (Artificial General Intelligence) research.

---

### **Key Themes in 2024**
- **Ethics & Safety**: Growing emphasis on mitigating risks (e.g., adversarial attacks, misuse) alongside innovation.
- **Interdisciplinary Impact**: AI's role in scientific discovery, healthcare, and climate solutions expanded rapidly.
- **Regulatory Pressure**: Governments and institutions began drafting frameworks to govern AI development, reflecting concerns about societal and existential risks.

For further details, refer to the original reports from sources like [Forbes](https://www.forbes.com), [Google Research](https://research.google), and the [Future of Life Institute](https://futureoflife.org).
